OpenRef features three key advancements: 1) Diverse visual scenarios: spanning diverse visual domains, including ground views, drone views, dark scenes and adverse weather conditions; 2) Variable target counts: breaking the single-target limitation with multi-target and none-target samples; 3) Rich vocabulary types: incorporating proper nouns, polysemous words and ordinal terms to fit a wider range of expression needs. Furthermore, as traditional metrics are insufficient for open-world setting, we leverage F1 to measure grounding accuracy and propose N3R (Negative Relative Rejection Reliability) to assess relative rejection reliability against negative expressions. Finally, we introduce Multi-task Consistency Checker (MCC), a training-free but plug-and-play strategy that enhances model performance with one click by enforcing consistency self-verification. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this work significantly advances the performance of existing REC models in complex scenarios, paving the way for open-world REC. Project page: https://zongjianwu.github.io/openref

</details>


### [145] [CMAP: Cross-Modal Adaptive Prompting for Multi-Domain Task-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25708)
*Sriram Mandalika*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于冻结CLIP模型的多领域任务增量学习方法，通过文本空间任务路由、多原型视觉-文本置信度估计和对称跨模态门控三个创新点，在MTIL基准上以仅2.5M可训练参数超越先前SOTA 3.0-5.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于冻结视觉-语言模型的多领域任务增量学习方法仅依赖视觉特征进行任务路由、置信度估计和编码器适配，完全未利用CLIP的跨模态文本嵌入空间，存在信息利用不充分的问题。

Method: 1) 文本空间任务路由：用冻结石文本原型的余弦相似度替代视觉高斯匹配，实现与顺序无关且对数据稀疏鲁棒的路由；2) 多原型视觉-文本置信度：用K-means视觉原型和跨模态对齐分数替代单高斯类建模，结合任务校准阈值；3) 对称跨模态门控：将逐层Gumbel门控扩展至文本编码器，以批次图像特征为条件，保持跨模态对齐。

Result: 在涵盖11个数据集、1201个类别的MTIL基准上，Order-I设置下达到74.2% Transfer、80.5% Average和88.7% Last，超越先前SOTA分别达5.0、3.7和3.0个百分点，仅使用2.5M可训练参数且无外部数据。

Conclusion: 充分利用CLIP跨模态文本嵌入空间能显著提升多领域任务增量学习性能，为参数高效方法提供了新方向。

Abstract: Multi-domain task-incremental learning requires a model to sequentially acquire knowledge across visually diverse domains without forgetting prior tasks, and without access to task identity at inference. Parameter-efficient methods built on frozen vision-language models have made strong progress, yet all existing approaches rely exclusively on visual features for task routing, confidence estimation, and encoder adaptation, leaving CLIP's cross-modal text embedding space entirely unexploited. We address this gap through three contributions. Text-space task routing replaces visual Gaussian matching with cosine similarity to frozen CLIP text prototypes, giving order-independent routing robust to data scarcity at zero parameter cost. Multi-prototype visual-textual confidence replaces single-Gaussian class modeling with K-means visual prototypes and cross-modal alignment scores under task-calibrated thresholds. Symmetric cross-modal gating extends per-layer Gumbel gates to the text encoder conditioned on batch image features, preserving cross-modal alignment on out-of-distribution inputs. On the MTIL benchmark spanning 11 datasets and 1201 classes, our method achieves 74.2% Transfer, 80.5% Average, and 88.7% Last under Order-I, surpassing the prior state of the art by 5.0, 3.7, and 3.0 percentage points with only 2.5M trainable parameters and no external data.

</details>


### [146] [TriDP-PTM: a three-stage distortion-perception tradeoff guides the pre-training model for radar cardiac sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25725)
*Jinye Li,Aidong Men,Yang Liu,Qingchao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TriDP-PTM，一种三阶段失真-感知预训练模型，通过雷达-心电图-任务双路径框架，在非接触式心脏监测中平衡失真与感知损失，在合作竞争阶段实现最佳临床下游准确率（波形分割mIoU 0.80，分类准确率98.3%，血压回归MAE降低56%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非接触式雷达心脏监测方法通常采用单一的"失真驱动"或"感知驱动"范式，面临"低失真但弱语义信息"与"高感知保真度但可解释性差"之间的权衡问题，亟需一种能兼顾两者优势的方法。

Method: 提出三阶段失真-感知预训练模型（TriDP-PTM），构建雷达-心电图-任务间接路径与雷达-任务直接路径的双路径多尺度融合框架，通过集成ECG生成器与特征判别器形成复合损失函数，将ECG形态与节律等医学先验引入下游任务。

Result: 在30名受试者5种生理状态的数据集上，间接路径优于直接路径：波形分割mIoU达0.80，四项分类任务平均准确率98.3%，血压回归MAE相比最强基线降低56%。

Conclusion: 在雷达-ECG间接路径中，适当权衡失真与感知损失使其处于合作竞争阶段，是同时获得临床可解释的ECG形态和强大下游准确性的关键。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases (CVDs) remain a leading cause of death globally, necessitating continuous, accurate non-invasive cardiac monitoring. While non-contact radar-based approaches show great promise, they often employ a single "distortion-driven" or "perception-driven" paradigm, frequently facing a trade-off between "low distortion but weak semantic information" and "high perceptual fidelity but poor interpretability." To address this, we propose a Three-stage Distortion-Perception Pre-Training Model (TriDP-PTM), a radar-based multi-scale fusion dual-path framework that systematically compares the "direct radar-to-task" path against an "indirect radar-to-ECG-to-task" path. By integrating an ECG generator with a feature discriminator to form a composite loss function, our approach effectively incorporates medical priors - such as ECG morphology and rhythm - into downstream tasks. Through empirical analysis, we reveal that this trade-off manifests in three distinct phases (Positive-Sum, Coopetitive, and Negative-Sum), showing optimal downstream clinical accuracy typically emerges in the coopetitive stage. Extensive experiments on a dataset involving 30 subjects across 5 physiological states reveal that the indirect path consistently outperforms the direct path in diverse tasks, achieving 0.80 mean IoU in waveform segmentation, 98.3% average classification accuracy across four tasks, and a 56% MAE reduction in blood pressure regression compared to the strongest baselines. These findings validate our framework and indicate that, within the indirect radar-to-ECG pathway, appropriately weighting distortion and perception losses to operate in the coopetitive regime is critical for achieving both clinically interpretable ECG morphology and strong downstream accuracy in non-contact cardiac monitoring.

</details>


### [147] [DeCoDrift: Stabilizing Decoder Coupling in Closed-Loop Foundation Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25730)
*H. M. Shadman Tabib,Md. Shamsuzzoha Bayzid,M Sohel Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现SAM等基础分割模型在迭代反馈循环中存在一种被忽视的失效模式——解码器耦合漂移，即掩码解码器的交叉注意力与目标物体逐渐失对齐，导致误差累积。作者提出DeCoDrift，一种无需训练和真值标注的推理时稳定化框架，通过约束提示更新和保持解码器耦合来提升迭代分割质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代式提示分割已成为常见做法（将预测掩码作为下一轮提示），但这种闭环动态过程中解码器层面的行为尚未得到系统研究，存在潜在的失效风险。

Method: 1) 通过仪器化SAM掩码解码器，推导出无需真值的提示-图像耦合度、注意力稳定性和时序一致性度量；2) 将迭代提示形式化为离散时间动力系统，分析近端锚定如何减少误差放大；3) 提出DeCoDrift框架，在推理时约束提示更新并维持解码器耦合。

Result: 在体电子显微镜数据上，标准迭代提示系统性地降低了注意力对齐和时序一致性；DeCoDrift在无需重训练或真值监督的情况下，持续提升了注意力稳定性、时序一致性和分割质量。

Conclusion: 解码器内部动态不仅是诊断性的，还能为闭环使用中的基础分割模型提供可操作的稳定化信号。

Abstract: Foundation segmentation models such as Segment Anything Model (SAM) are now routinely used in iterative pipelines, where each predicted mask is fed back as the next prompt. This practice turns segmentation into a closed-loop dynamical process, yet the decoder-level behavior of these systems remains largely unexamined. We show that this feedback loop can induce a previously overlooked failure mode, decoder coupling drift, in which the mask decoder's cross-attention progressively loses alignment with the target object, causing errors to accumulate across iterations. We study this phenomenon by instrumenting SAM's mask decoder and deriving ground-truth-free measures of prompt-image coupling, attention stability, and temporal consistency. On volumetric electron microscopy data, these decoder-internal signals reveal that standard iterative prompting systematically degrades attention alignment and temporal coherence relative to oracle-anchored feedback. We then formalize iterative prompting as a discrete-time dynamical system and show how proximal anchoring reduces error amplification in the feedback loop. Building on this analysis, we introduce DeCoDrift, a training-free inference-time stabilization framework that constrains prompt updates and preserves decoder coupling across iterations. Across extensive experiments, DeCoDrift consistently improves attention stability, temporal coherence, and segmentation quality over standard iterative prompting, without retraining or ground-truth supervision. More broadly, our results show that decoder-internal dynamics are not merely diagnostic: they provide actionable signals for stabilizing foundation segmentation models in closed-loop use.

</details>


### [148] [Broadband Hyperspectral 3D Imaging using Dispersed Structured Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25757)
*Suhyun Shin,Yunseong Moon,Ryota Maeda,David Lindell,Kyros Kutulacos,Seung-Hwan Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种宽带高光谱3D成像方法(BH3D)，利用单光谱仪和可见光-SWIR立体相机系统，在450-1500nm宽谱段内同时重建高光谱反射率和精确的3D几何信息，并验证了其在材料识别、透过不透明层成像等应用中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高光谱3D成像局限于可见光窄谱段，而宽谱段（可见光到短波红外）能同时捕获表面外观和亚表面物性信息，但现有传感器（硅基vs InGaAs）差异导致系统设计复杂，亟需一种简化的宽带高光谱3D成像方案。

Method: 采用单光谱仪设计，构建可见光与SWIR相机构成的立体系统；将色散结构光扩展到宽带场景，并建立宽带色散结构光的图像形成模型，联合估计高光谱反射率和深度信息。

Result: 在真实场景中实现平均光谱角0.13 rad、均方根误差0.03、平均深度误差4.5 mm的精准重建；成功展示了同色异谱材料识别、透过不透明层成像、钞票隐藏特征揭示及血管成像等应用。

Conclusion: 提出的单光谱仪宽带高光谱3D成像系统有效克服了传感器约束，在宽谱段内实现了光谱与几何信息的高质量联合重建，拓展了高光谱3D成像在材料分析和生物医学等领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Hyperspectral 3D imaging enables the capture of dense spectral information and scene geometry but has traditionally been confined to narrow spectral windows, typically the visible range. In this work, we introduce a broadband hyperspectral 3D imaging (BH3D) method to extend this capability across the full visible-near-infrared and short-wavelength infrared (SWIR) spectrum (450-1500 nm). This broad coverage is critical as it captures complementary physical cues: visible wavelengths reveal surface appearance, while SWIR bands provide insight into subsurface properties and material composition. However, realizing BH3D is challenging due to fundamental sensor constraints between visible-spectrum silicon and SWIR-spectrum InGaAs sensors, which necessitate complex multi-spectrograph designs. Here we propose a single-spectrograph BH3D system, using a stereo setup comprising visible and SWIR cameras, that reconstructs dense broadband hyperspectral reflectance together with accurate 3D geometry. Our key idea is to extend dispersed structured light to the broadband regime using a single spectrograph. We model the image formation of broadband dispersed structured light, and estimate hyperspectral reflectance and depth. We validate our approach on diverse real-world scenes, demonstrating accurate reconstruction with a mean spectral angle mapper of 0.13 rad, root mean square error of 0.03, and mean depth error of 4.5 mm. We further demonstrate identifying metameric materials, performing imaging through opaque layers, uncovering hidden features on banknotes, and revealing blood vessels.

</details>


### [149] [Towards Anatomically Plausible Human Image Generation via Synthetic Localized Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25759)
*Bao Li,Yuliang Xiu,Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对文本到图像基础模型生成人体图像时解剖结构错误的问题，提出ASAP框架，通过构造受控偏好对和局部化偏好优化算法，有效减少解剖错误并保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型生成人体图像时解剖结构错误频发；监督微调依赖高质量人体照片数据集，但此类数据有限且受光照、姿态、背景等混杂因素干扰导致优化信号不明确；标准DPO无法利用解剖伪影的局部性特征。

Method: 1) 提出局部退化机制，在高保真人体图像的目标区域引入显式解剖错误，构建受控偏好对；2) 基于此创建Human Anatomical Preference (HAP)数据集；3) 提出局部化、带边界的DPO变体，优先优化目标解剖区域并设置有限偏好边界防止过度优化；4) 提出HAF-Bench基准评估解剖保真度。

Result: ASAP在多个基础模型上持续减少解剖错误，同时保持整体图像质量。

Conclusion: 通过构造受控局部偏好对和针对性优化算法，可以有效解决人体图像生成中的解剖结构问题，且不牺牲整体图像质量。

Abstract: Large-scale text-to-image foundation models have achieved remarkable visual realism, yet generating human images with correct anatomical structures remains challenging. Existing approaches enforce anatomical constraints through part-specific modules or localized loss weighting during supervised fine-tuning on high-quality human photos, but such datasets are limited and often provide ambiguous optimization signals due to confounding factors such as lighting, pose, and background. Preference-based alignment offers an alternative, but standard Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) treats all pixels equally and therefore fails to exploit the localized nature of anatomical artifacts. To address this, we propose the framework of Alignment via Synthetic Anatomical Preference (ASAP), which constructs controlled preference pairs through a localized degradation mechanism applied to high-fidelity human images. This mechanism performs a controlled experiment on images by introducing explicit anatomical errors in targeted regions while preserving the remaining content. With this mechanism, we create the Human Anatomical Preference (HAP) dataset with over 10K curated pairs for effective anatomical alignment of text-to-image human image generative models. To better leverage the locality of these controlled preference pairs, we introduce a localized and margin-bounded variant of DPO that prioritizes optimization in targeted anatomical regions while enforcing a finite preference margin to prevent over-optimization and preserve global semantics. We further introduce HAF-Bench, a benchmark for systematic evaluation of anatomical fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ASAP consistently reduces anatomical errors across multiple foundation models while maintaining overall image quality.

</details>


### [150] [AI-T2I: Aggregating-and-Isolating Cross-Attention to Diffusion Models for Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25763)
*Shipeng Cao,Biao Qian,Haipeng Liu,Yang Wang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AI-T2I方法，通过聚合损失解决同主体内token激活散乱问题，并通过隔离损失推远不同主体间token激活，从而提升文本到图像生成的跨注意力对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型在跨注意力图中难以实现精确的文本-图像对齐，现有工作主要关注不同主体token之间的激活重叠问题，而忽略了同一主体内部token激活的散乱问题。

Method: 提出聚合-隔离交叉注意力方法（AI-T2I）：1）聚合损失用于识别并整合散乱的同主体内token激活，同时隐式缓解潜在的重叠问题；2）隔离损失进一步将不同主体间token激活推离，实现精确对齐。

Result: 在多个基准上超越现有最先进方法，并在可控布局生成和个性化生成等下游任务中表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: AI-T2I有效解决了跨注意力中同主体散乱和异主体重叠的双重问题，显著提升了文本到图像生成的对齐质量和泛化性能。

Abstract: Text-to-image synthesis has made significant progress, benefiting from the strong generative capabilities of diffusion models. However, these models struggle to achieve precise text-to-image alignment within cross-attention maps during the denoising process. Existing works primarily focus on inter-subject-token activations (i.e., cross-attention scores) overlap for different subjects, overlooking the intra-subject-token activations scattering issue for identical subjects. In this paper, we propose an Aggregating-and-Isolating cross-attention approach to diffusion models for Text-to-Image synthesis, dubbed AI-T2I. Technically, to address the scattering issue, we devise an aggregation loss to identify and consolidate the scattered intra-token activations, which implicitly helps mitigate the potential overlap issue. Upon that, an isolation loss is further introduced to push the inter-token activations apart, thus fulfilling precise text-to-image alignment. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of AI-T2I over the state-of-the-art works for text-to-image synthesis. Furthermore, our AI-T2I exhibits excellent generalization across other tasks, e.g., controllable layout generation and personalized generation.

</details>


### [151] [Benchmarking Pathology Foundation Models for Spatial Domain Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25764)
*Bokai Zhao,Yiyang Zhang,Yuanchi Zhu,Hanqing Chao,Long Bai,Tai Ma,Minfeng Xu,Ming Song,Tianzi Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpaPath-Bench是一个专门诊断病理基础模型空间表征能力的基准测试，通过空间域识别任务评估模型在WSI中区分组织区域和捕获空间关系的能力，涵盖19个编码器和7种方法，在83K次运行中揭示了不同预训练范式对组织空间架构的编码差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理基础模型评估主要依赖下游临床任务，无法揭示模型表征本身是否编码了有意义的组织区域及其空间关系，缺乏对空间表征能力的直接诊断工具。

Method: 构建SpaPath-Bench基准，基于配对的WSI和空间转录组学数据，将空间域识别作为诊断任务；整合42张公共WSI-ST配对切片，评估19种编码器和7种SDI方法；使用无监督空间一致性、转录组参考一致性和专家参考一致性三个互补标准衡量分区质量。

Result: 通过83,000+次运行实验，揭示了不同预训练范式（如对比学习、掩码自编码等）对组织空间架构的不同编码特性，为构建下一代空间感知病理计算模型提供了实践指导。

Conclusion: SpaPath-Bench填补了病理基础模型空间表征能力评估的空白，能够有效诊断模型对组织区域及其空间关系的编码能力，为改进模型设计提供了可操作的洞见。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) have emerged as a core approach for learning transferable representations from whole slide images (WSIs), and they are typically benchmarked through downstream clinical endpoints. While such task level evaluations are indispensable, they offer limited insight into what the representations themselves encode, particularly whether PFM embeddings can distinguish meaningful tissue regions and capture their spatial relationships. We present SpaPath-Bench, a representation level benchmark designed to diagnose spatial representation capability in PFMs. SpaPath-Bench formulates spatial domain identification (SDI) on paired whole slide image and spatial transcriptomics (ST) data as a diagnostic task. It curates 42 public paired WSI and ST slides, enables large scale evaluation across 19 encoders and seven SDI methods, and measures partition quality using three complementary criteria: unsupervised spatial coherence, transcriptomics referenced agreement, and expert referenced agreement. Across 83K runs, SpaPath-Bench reveals that different pretraining paradigms capture distinct aspects of tissue spatial architecture, and it provides practical guidance for building the next generation of spatially aware computational pathology models. Code and data pipelines are publicly available at https://bokai-zhao.github.io/SpaPath-benchboard/.

</details>


### [152] [Concept Unlearning via Cross-Attention Activation Projection for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25765)
*Saemi Moon,Suhyeon Jun,Seoyeon Lee,Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PURE是一种基于跨注意力激活空间投影的闭式概念擦除方法，通过在去噪轨迹中捕获每层交叉注意力激活来构建遗忘和保留基，对键值权重施加线性投影，在保持保留概念的同时有效防止目标概念在释义和对抗性提示下的泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭式方法通过文本编码器对少数锚定提示的响应来表示目标概念，但释义提示可轻易绕过编辑。作者认为目标应表示在交叉注意力激活空间中，因为文本嵌入描述用户提示，而交叉注意力激活描述模型即将渲染的内容，后者能泛化到模板未覆盖的释义。

Method: PURE：从短去噪轨迹中捕获每层交叉注意力激活，构建遗忘基和保留基，然后对交叉注意力的键和值权重施加单个线性投影器。

Result: 在涵盖艺术风格、知识产权、名人及NSFW等十个概念的整体基准上，PURE在释义和对抗性提示下显著降低目标泄漏，同时保持保留概念接近未编辑模型，在遗忘-保留权衡上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 在交叉注意力激活空间中表示目标概念比文本嵌入空间更有效地泛化到释义提示，线性投影操作可实现更好的遗忘-保留权衡。

Abstract: Concept unlearning aims to erase a target concept from a pretrained text-to-image diffusion model without retraining. Closed-form methods are attractive in this setting because they apply a single deterministic edit to the cross-attention weights and add no inference-time cost. Existing closed-form methods, however, represent the target concept through the text encoder's response to a few short anchor prompts that name it, and paraphrased prompts that evoke the concept without naming it consistently bypass the edit. We argue that the target should instead be represented in the cross-attention activation space. Text embeddings describe the user's prompt, while cross-attention activations describe what the model is about to render, and the latter generalize to paraphrase the anchor templates do not cover. Building on this observation, we propose PURE (Projection in U-Net Rendering for Erasure), a closed-form method that builds the forget and retain bases from per-layer cross-attention activations captured along a short denoising trajectory and applies a single linear projector to the cross-attention key and value weights. On a recent holistic concept-unlearning benchmark covering ten concepts across artistic style, intellectual property, celebrity, and NSFW categories, PURE significantly reduces target leakage under paraphrased and adversarial prompts while preserving retain concepts close to the unedited model, yielding the best overall forget-retain trade-off among evaluated methods.

</details>


### [153] [SAFE-Diff: Scale-Aware Attention and Feature-Dispersive Diffusion with Uncertainty Estimation for Contrast-Enhanced Breast MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25767)
*Tianyu Zhang,Xinglong Liang,Jarek van Dijk,Luyi Han,Chunyao Lu,Antonio Portaluri,Xinghe Xie,Yaofei Duan,Nika Rasoolzadeh,Xin Wang,Yuan Gao,Muzhen He,Yue Sun,Jonas Teuwen,Tao Tan,Ritse Mann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了合成高保真度对比增强MRI在乳腺癌筛查中的临床价值与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现更安全、更高效的乳腺癌筛查，需要合成高保真度的对比增强MRI，但病变纹理复杂和增强模式异质性是主要难点。

Method: 未在摘要中明确阐述具体方法。

Result: 未在摘要中明确阐述具体结果。

Conclusion: 合成高保真对比增强MRI具有重要临床价值，但仍面临病灶纹理复杂和增强模式异质性等挑战。

Abstract: Synthesizing high fidelity contrast enhanced MRI is clinically valuable for safer and more efficient breast cancer screening, yet remains challenging due to complex lesion textures and heterogeneous enhancement patterns.

</details>


### [154] [DRFusion: Drift-Resilient Temporally Consistent Infrared-Visible Video Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25775)
*Xingyuan Li,Haoyuan Xu,Shulin Li,Xiang Chen,Zhiying Jiang,Jinyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种抗漂移的红外与可见光视频融合方法，将任务重构为基于历史条件的运动生成，通过稳定历史引导、软时间锚定和解耦结构-运动适应策略，实现了先进水平的融合质量和时间稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外-可见光视频融合方法存在两个核心问题：(1) 基于光流的方法几何刚性且易产生重影伪影；(2) 基于扩散的帧级融合模型扩展至自回归设置时缺乏时序约束，导致误差累积和漂移现象。

Method: (1) 将视频融合重构为历史条件运动生成任务；(2) 稳定历史引导与软时间锚定：将时序一致性重新定义为频谱滤波，隐式聚合运动动态而无需刚性对齐；(3) 解耦结构-运动适应策略：通过两阶段训练和潜在空间精调，桥接预训练先验与结构约束。

Result: 在融合质量和时间稳定性方面均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过运动生成视角和隐式时序约束机制，有效解决了视频融合中的误差累积与漂移问题，实现了鲁棒的时序一致性。

Abstract: Infrared and visible video fusion is essential for achieving comprehensive perception in dynamic scenes. However, maintaining temporal consistency remains a formidable challenge. Conventional methods relying on optical flow often suffer from geometric rigidity and ghosting artifacts. Moreover, standard diffusion-based fusion models typically operate in a frame-by-frame manner; when extended to autoregressive settings, they lack intrinsic temporal constraints and are prone to severe error accumulation and drifting, where minor artifacts amplify over time. To address these limitations, we propose a drift-resilient video fusion method that reformulates the task as history-conditioned motion generation. We introduce Stabilized History Guidance and Soft Temporal Anchoring to reframe temporal consistency as spectral filtering, implicitly aggregating motion dynamics without rigid alignment. Furthermore, our Decoupled Structure-Motion Adaptation strategy bridges pre-trained priors and structural constraints via two-stage training and latent refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both fusion quality and temporal stability.

</details>


### [155] [OMGTex: One-stage Multi-style Facial Texture Reconstruction without Geometry Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25778)
*Zitong Xiao,Yuda Qiu,Zisheng Ye,Xiaoguang Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OMGTex是一个基于扩散模型的端到端框架，无需3D几何先验即可从多风格人脸图像重建高质量、可编辑的面部UV纹理，并提出了梯度引导的推理策略和语义感知训练范式，同时构建了首个涵盖真实与风格化域的综合配对纹理数据集CANVAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纹理重建方法存在两个主要局限：(1) 依赖3D几何先验导致脆弱性，尤其在面部遮挡或风格化域中难以准确估计；(2) 缺乏语义解耦能力，无法进行区域特定的纹理编辑和风格迁移。

Method: 提出几何无关管线，直接将2D人脸图像映射到可编辑UV纹理。关键技术包括：(1) 推理时梯度引导的细化策略，纠正扩散生成中的UV未对齐问题；(2) 利用扩散模型的语义分布能力，设计新的训练范式增强语义感知编辑能力。同时构建了CANVAS数据集。

Result: 在多个面部纹理基准上达到了最先进的性能，实现了跨多样域鲁棒、风格一致且可编辑的面部纹理重建。

Conclusion: OMGTex是首个几何无关的推理框架，能够在不同域中实现鲁棒、风格一致且可编辑的面部纹理重建，同时解决了数据稀缺问题。

Abstract: We propose OMGTex, an end-to-end diffusion-based framework for reconstructing high-quality and editable facial UV textures from multi-style facial images. Existing texture reconstruction methods face two major limitations: (1) Fragility due to reliance on 3D geometry priors, which are difficult to estimate accurately, especially under facial occlusions or in stylized domains; and (2) A lack of semantic disentanglement, inhibiting region-specific texture editing and style transfer. Our work addresses both challenges simultaneously.
  Our core innovation is a geometry-free pipeline that directly maps a 2D face image to its corresponding editable UV texture. We introduce two key techniques: First, to address the challenge of UV misalignment common in diffusion generation, we introduce a gradient-guided refinement strategy at inference time, which explicitly corrects structural consistency. Second, we leverage the inherent semantic distribution capability of diffusion models and design a novel training paradigm to enhance this tendency, enabling semantic-aware editing of facial texture. Furthermore, to address the data scarcity in multi-style texture reconstruction, we construct CANVAS, the first comprehensive paired texture reconstruction dataset covering realistic and diverse stylized domains.
  To the best of our knowledge, OMGTex is the first geometry-free inference framework that achieves robust, style-consistent, and editable facial texture reconstruction across diverse domains. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple facial texture benchmarks.

</details>


### [156] [VertiCue-Bench: Diagnosing Whether MLLMs Use Height Cues to Resolve 2D Ambiguity in Remote Sensing Natural Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25784)
*Jing Huang,Duanchu Wang,Junjie Yang,Zihang Cheng,Cheng Li,Lin Cui,Zhouyi Wu,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VertiCue-Bench是一个针对多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在遥感地理空间推理中能否利用垂直结构信息(如冠层高度模型CHM)消解光谱混淆的基准测试。评估14个模型发现：模型能读取低层高度线索，但无法将几何感知转化为可靠的语义推理，揭示了几何到语义的显著鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感基准多集中于2D光学外观评估，但在自然环境中生态上不同的区域常因光谱混淆(相似纹理但垂直结构不同)而难以区分。当前MLLMs是否真正利用垂直线索来解决外观层面的歧义尚不清楚。

Method: 构建VertiCue-Bench，包含1,534个精心策划的样本，覆盖17个任务，明确解耦低层高度感知与歧义感知的语义推理。对14个SOTA通用和遥感专用MLLMs进行反事实模态测试。

Result: 模型在读取原始CHM高度线索方面表现出涌现能力，但在将几何感知转化为可靠语义推理时大多失败，在需要联合约束时甚至不如仅基于RGB的基线模型。

Conclusion: 揭示了自然场景理解中存在的关键几何到语义的鸿沟(geometry-to-semantics gap)，为推进地理空间MLLMs提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have recently shown promising progress in geospatial reasoning. However, existing remote sensing benchmarks remain largely 2D-centric, evaluating models primarily on optical appearance. In natural environments, this paradigm breaks down due to severe spectral confusion, where ecologically distinct regions share similar textures but differ fundamentally in vertical structure. In such cases, explicit 3D structural data, such as Canopy Height Models (CHMs), become essential geometric evidence for semantic disambiguation. Yet, it remains unclear whether current MLLMs can genuinely leverage vertical cues to resolve appearance-level ambiguity. To address this gap, we introduce VertiCue-Bench, the first diagnostic benchmark for CHM-grounded geospatial reasoning. VertiCue-Bench comprises 1,534 carefully curated instances across 17 tasks, explicitly disentangling low-level height perception from ambiguity-aware semantic reasoning. Evaluations on 14 state-of-the-art general and remote-sensing-specialized MLLMs, combined with counterfactual modality testing, reveal a striking perception-reasoning dissociation. While models exhibit emerging competence in reading raw CHM height cues, they largely fail to translate geometric perception into reliable semantic reasoning, often underperforming RGB-only baselines when joint constraints are required. Overall, VertiCue-Bench exposes a critical geometry-to-semantics gap in natural scene understanding, offering actionable insights for advancing geospatial MLLMs.

</details>


### [157] [Addressing Exacerbated Attention Sink for Source-Free Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25799)
*Shuai Yi,Yixiong Zou,Yuhua Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现跨域小样本学习（CDFSL）中，标准目标域微调会显著加剧注意力沉没（attention sink）问题，导致模型跨类判别能力下降。该问题源于模型为克服域间差距而采取的捷径学习——倾向于让距离目标域类别更近的“简单token”进一步靠近该类，忽视“困难token”的学习。为此，提出动态重加权token的方法（TIR），抑制简单token依赖、增强困难token学习，减少沉没问题并提升判别性，在四个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往工作未关注到CDFSL场景中注意力沉没现象被显著加剧的问题，导致模型判别能力下降，限制了VLM在跨域小样本场景下的潜力。

Method: 提出TIR方法，在目标域微调过程中根据token与目标域类别的相关性动态重加权token，抑制模型对简单token的过度依赖，增强对困难token的学习，从而减少注意力沉没token并提升判别性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上验证了方法合理性，取得了新的最优性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 揭示了CDFSL中注意力沉没现象的加剧机制及其作为域适应捷径学习的本质，证明了动态token重加权策略在该场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) like CLIP have shown impressive generalization capabilities, yet their potential for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning (CDFSL) remains underexplored, where the model needs to transfer source-domain information to target domains with scarce training data. While the attention sink phenomenon has been observed in VLMs for certain tasks, its role in CDFSL scenarios has not been studied. In this paper, we uncover a critical issue overlooked by prior works: standard target-domain few-shot fine-tuning in CDFSL significantly exacerbates the attention sink problem, leading to poor discriminability across classes. To understand this phenomenon, through extensive experiments, we interpret it as the model's shortcut learning for domain adaptation: to overcome the huge domain gap between the source and target domains, the model shows a high tendency to push tokens that are initially closer to target-domain classes (i.e., simple tokens) to be even closer to these classes, exacerbating the attention sink and wasting the capability of learning other discriminative but initially further tokens (i.e., hard tokens). To address this, we propose a novel approach to dynamically re-weight tokens according to their relevance with target-domain classes during the target-domain finetuning, which explicitly suppresses the model's reliance on these simple tokens and enhances the learning of hard tokens, reducing sink tokens and enhancing discriminability. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets validate the rationale of our method, demonstrating new state-of-the-art performance. Our codes are available at https://github.com/shuaiyi308/TIR.

</details>


### [158] [PixelWizard: Towards Efficient High-Fidelity Video Generation at Ultra-Large Spatial Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25801)
*Wenxue Li,Jingjing Ren,Peng Zhang,Tian Ye,Daiguo Zhou,Jian Luan,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixelWizard通过层次化解耦全局结构建模与细节合成，并引入噪声跨度对齐的捷径训练，在保持结构稳定性的同时实现2K/4K视频生成的10倍以上加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率视频生成面临优化不稳定和计算成本高昂的双重瓶颈：token序列的大规模扩展导致优化偏向局部纹理而破坏全局一致性，同时带来高昂的训练成本和严重的推理延迟。

Method: 提出PixelWizard框架：(1) 紧凑时空锚点层次化解耦全局结构与细节合成；(2) 噪声跨度对齐捷径训练，通过显式建模步长实现大步长轨迹遍历；(3) 指数索引偏置采样与自适应噪声跨度校准，对齐高分辨率网格的偏移噪声调度。

Result: 在原生2K/4K视频生成任务中，实现10倍以上的生成采样加速，同时达到优越的视觉质量。

Conclusion: PixelWizard有效解决了高分辨率视频生成中结构坍塌与推理延迟问题，在不依赖蒸馏的情况下实现了稳健的少步推理。

Abstract: High-resolution video generation faces a coupled bottleneck of optimization instability and prohibitive computational costs. The massive expansion of the token sequence not only biases optimization toward local textures at the expense of global coherence, leading to structural collapse, but also imposes prohibitive training costs and severe inference latency. To address this, we propose PixelWizard, a framework that hierarchically decouples global structure modeling from fine-grained detail synthesis. PixelWizard first establishes a compact spatiotemporal anchor to concentrate dense structural priors, which then guides fine-grained generation at high resolution. This mitigates the local optimization bias to ensure structural stability without compromising high-frequency details. Leveraging this structural stability, we introduce Noise-Span Aligned Shortcut Training to break the inference bottleneck. By explicitly modeling the step size, this mechanism allows the model to traverse the generation trajectory with large steps. Crucially, we incorporate Exponential Index-Biased Sampling and Adaptive Noise-Span Calibration to align optimization with the shifted noise schedules of high-resolution grids, ensuring robust few-step inference without incurring the heavy overhead of distillation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PixelWizard achieves superior visual quality while accelerating the generative sampling of native 2K/4K videos by over 10x.

</details>


### [159] [Rethinking VLM Representation for VLA Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25802)
*Weifeng Lin,Siyuan Huang,Hao Li,Tingwei Chen,Ruichuan An,Xinyu Wei,Jianbo Liu,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了VLA初始化中预训练VLM表示的作用，发现：(1) 原始VLM表示是动作性能的关键来源；(2) 具身VQA适配收益不均，不同能力域的增益不可简单叠加；(3) LoRA比全量微调更可靠，过度重塑预训练表示会削弱初始化效果；(4) 机器人数据预训练可进一步提升性能，分阶段LoRA训练方案最优。有效VLM-to-VLA适配应在注入动作相关信号的同时保留预训练VLM表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型广泛采用预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）作为策略骨干，但何种预训练VLM表示对VLA初始化有用尚不明确，本文旨在系统探究这一问题。

Method: 将VLA初始化作为受控的表示设计问题，从三个维度进行研究：能力级具身VQA监督、参数更新策略、机器人数据预训练。通过对比实验分析不同初始化方式对动作性能的影响。

Result: (1) 原始预训练VLM表示是动作性能的关键来源；(2) 具身VQA适配收益非均匀，依赖于下游瓶颈，不同能力域增益不可简单叠加；(3) LoRA比全量微调提供更可靠的初始化，过度重塑预训练表示会削弱初始化效果；(4) 机器人数据预训练可进一步提升VLA初始化，分阶段LoRA训练方案最优。

Conclusion: 有效的VLM-to-VLA适配应在注入动作相关的具身和机器人轨迹信号的同时，保留对动作学习仍有用的预训练VLM表示。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models widely adopt pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as policy backbones, yet it remains unclear what kind of pretrained VLM representation is useful as a VLA initialization. In this paper, we study VLA initialization as a controlled representation-design problem along three axes: capability-level embodied VQA supervision, parameter-update strategy, and robot-data pretraining. Our experiments show that the original pretrained VLM representation is a key source of action performance. However, embodied VQA adaptation does not yield uniform gains: its benefit depends on downstream bottlenecks, and gains from different capability domains are not simply additive. For update strategy, LoRA provides a more reliable initialization than Full Finetune, indicating that overly reshaping the pretrained representation can weaken VLA initialization. Robot-data pretraining further improves VLA initialization, with the strongest variant obtained by staged LoRA-based training. Together, these findings suggest that effective VLM-to-VLA adaptation should inject action-relevant embodied and robot-trajectory signals while preserving the pretrained VLM representation that remains useful for action learning.

</details>


### [160] [ATV-Net: Adaptive Triple-View Network with Dynamic Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25803)
*Hsin-Jui Pan,Sheng-Wei Chan,Meng-Qian Li,Chun-Po Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ATV-Net，通过仅修改分割头、使用三种互补感受野视图（微观、局部、侦察）并引入自适应决策门动态融合，使ResNet-101在Cityscapes上达到80.31% mIoU，证明经典CNN分割方法仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语义分割研究趋向于更强的上下文建模、密集注意力和Transformer架构，但这些方法复杂度高。作者希望探究在仅修改分割头的情况下，基于ResNet的经典CNN分割模型能提升到何种程度，以验证传统CNN流水线是否仍具实用价值。

Method: 提出ATV-Net：1) 三种互补感受野视图——微观视图（逐点语义响应）、局部视图（邻域结构与物体边界）、侦察视图（扩大上下文线索）；2) 自适应决策门，根据输入场景特征动态选择感受野响应；3) 紧凑的全局协调层，提升空间和语义一致性。

Result: 在Cityscapes验证集上达到80.31% mIoU。

Conclusion: 经典CNN分割方法远未过时：通过简单的感受野视图和自适应融合，基于ResNet的流水线无需Transformer式全局注意力或过于复杂的上下文模块，即可达到有竞争力的精度。

Abstract: Recent semantic segmentation research has increasingly moved toward stronger context modeling, dense attention, and transformer-based architectures. Although these models achieve impressive performance, classical CNN-based segmentation pipelines remain attractive because of their simplicity, efficiency, and ease of implementation. This paper revisits a practical question: how far can a ResNet-based segmentation model be improved by only modifying the segmentation head? We propose ATV-Net, an Adaptive Triple-View Network that strengthens a ResNet-101 backbone using three simple but complementary receptive-field views. The micro view captures point-wise semantic responses, the local view models neighborhood structures and object boundaries, and the scout view provides enlarged contextual cues. Instead of fusing these views with fixed weights, ATV-Net introduces an Adaptive Decision Gate that dynamically selects receptive-field responses according to input scene characteristics. A compact global coordination layer is further applied to improve spatial and semantic consistency. Experiments on the Cityscapes validation set show that ATV-Net achieves 80.31\% mIoU. This result suggests that classical CNN-based segmentation is still far from obsolete: with simple receptive-field views and adaptive fusion, a ResNet-based pipeline can reach a competitive accuracy level without relying on transformer-style global attention or overly complex context modules.

</details>


### [161] [Event-to-Video Reconstruction using Spatio-Temporal and Frequency-Enhanced Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25804)
*Ramna Maqsood,Paulo Nunes,Luís Ducla Soares,Caroline Conti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MSFET-E2V，一种多尺度频率增强的Transformer模型，通过跨域注意力模块融合时空特征与离散小波变换的频率表示，以及轻量级小波增强跳跃块，在事件到视频重建任务中实现了优于现有方法的性能，同时显著降低了参数量、GPU内存占用和推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高时间分辨率、低延迟和低功耗等优势，但缺乏密集强度帧限制了传统计算机视觉方法的直接应用。现有基于CNN和Transformer的E2V重建方法主要在空间域操作，难以恢复精细结构细节并抑制严重的重建伪影。

Method: 提出MSFET-E2V多尺度频率增强Transformer模型，核心是跨域注意力模块，融合时空特征与基于离散小波变换的频率感知表示，同时捕捉低高频成分的局部和全局结构。另提出轻量级小波增强跳跃块作为跳跃连接，通过联合空间-频率域处理抑制伪影并细化结构细节。

Result: 在多个真实世界事件数据集上，MSFET-E2V在重建质量上优于现有最先进方法。与基于Transformer的现有方法相比，显著减少了参数量、GPU内存占用和推理时间。

Conclusion: MSFET-E2V通过引入频率增强机制有效解决了现有E2V方法在空间域处理中细节恢复不足和伪影严重的问题，实现了更高效、更高质量的事件到视频重建。

Abstract: Event cameras offer significant advantages over conventional frame-based counterparts, including high temporal resolution, low latency, and energy efficiency. These characteristics make them suitable for high-speed and high-dynamic range scene acquisition scenarios; however, the lack of dense intensity frames limits the direct applicability of conventional computer vision methods for scene understanding. Event-to-video (E2V) reconstruction seeks to bridge this gap by converting asynchronous event streams into a sequence of synchronous video frames. Existing E2V reconstruction methods based on convolutional neural networks and transformers operate primarily in the spatial domain and often struggle to recover fine structural details while suppressing severe reconstruction artifacts. To address these issues, we propose MSFET-E2V, a novel multiscale frequency-enhanced transformer model. At its core lies a cross-domain attention module, which fuses spatio-temporal features with frequency-aware representations derived from the discrete wavelet transform. Unlike prior methods relying solely on spatial attention, our approach effectively captures both local and global structures by taking into account low- and high-frequency components, enhancing detail preservation and robustness across various motion scenarios. Furthermore, we propose a lightweight wavelet-enhanced skip block that serves as a skip connection, facilitating artifact suppression and structural detail refinement through joint spatial-frequency domain processing. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MSFET-E2V achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art methods on multiple real-world event datasets, offering significant gains in reconstruction quality. Moreover, compared to the existing transformer-based method, our proposed model significantly reduces the number of parameters, the GPU memory usage, and inference time.

</details>


### [162] [An Analysis Focused on Womens Safety: Can VAD Models Be Enhanced by a Multi-modal Dataset?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25806)
*Sangeeta,Maddikuntla Sai Prajwal,Debi Prosad Dogra,Kamalakar Vijay Thakare,Hyungjoo Jung,Ig-Jae Kim,Heeseung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ExtrAnom数据集，一个专注于女性安全的多模态视频异常检测基准，包含1001个带有文本描述的视频，涵盖5种针对女性的犯罪类型，并针对现有数据集在低光照、低分辨率、远距离拍摄等方面的不足进行了弥补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测数据集缺乏针对女性安全的专门关注，且存在光照条件好、分辨率高、特写镜头多等问题，无法有效代表针对女性的犯罪异常行为（如抢项链、跟踪、不当触摸等细微犯罪行为）。

Method: 构建ExtrAnom数据集，包含1001个视频（500正常，501异常），涵盖5种女性犯罪类型；视频包含低光照(8%)、低分辨率(13%)、远距离拍摄(15%)以及日光(64%)场景；每个视频配备4条文本注释（1条人工生成，3条大模型生成），支持跨模态和视觉语言模型验证。

Result: 在现有的单模态和多模态VAD数据集（如XD-Violence、UCF-Crime、UCA）以及SOTA方法上进行了基准测试，实验表明现有数据集不足以训练模型检测针对女性的异常行为。

Conclusion: ExtrAnom数据集能够更准确地检测针对女性的异常模式，并补充视觉语言模型生成视频级描述的能力，填补了女性安全视频异常检测领域的空白。

Abstract: Women's safety and security are paramount for a modern society. Crimes against women occur in daylight as well as in low-light conditions. Often, such events are captured through real-world surveillance cameras that operate at lower resolutions. Despite substantial progress in CV-related research, video anomaly detection (VAD) focused on women's safety has not yet been adequately addressed. Existing video anomaly datasets contain well-lit, high-resolution, close-shot videos, and fail to represent women-centric anomalies such as chain snatching, stalking, inappropriate touch, and other subtle forms of crime against women. To address these problems, we propose the ExtrAnom dataset, a new multi-modal benchmark containing 1001 videos with textual descriptions, 500 normal and 501 anomalous, classified into 5 different types of women-centric crimes. The dataset comprises low-light (8%), low-resolution videos (13%), long-shot (15%), along with daylight (64%) anomalous videos. And it covers anomalous events like stalking (3.9%), chain snatching (17.6%), kidnapping (7.3%), assassinations (2.3%), harassment (18.9%), and normal (50%). Each video is supplemented with 4 textual annotations, including one human-generated and three LLM-generated descriptions, enabling cross-modal and VLM-based validations. The aim of creating a women-centric dataset is to accurately detect the women-centric anomaly patterns, which are possible to observe visually. The dataset supplements the VLMs to accurately generate video-level descriptions. ExtrAnom has been benchmarked against popular unimodal and multi-modal VAD datasets (e.g., XD-Violence, UCF-Crime, and UCA) and SOTA methods. Experiments reveal that the existing datasets are insufficient to train models for detecting women-centric anomalies.

</details>


### [163] [Data-driven Head Motion Generation through Natural Gaze-Head Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25810)
*Xiaohan Liu,Yilin Wen,Yusuke Sugano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个数据驱动的 gaze-head 协调时序建模方法，利用大规模野外面部视频，通过自动流程提取自然注视/头部运动，基于条件变分自编码器生成与注视相关的头部运动，并应用于注视控制的面部视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对视-头协调（gaze-head coordination）的时序建模，尤其是缺乏大规模数据驱动的通用模型，且未充分关注注视与头部运动的自然相关性在视频生成中的应用。

Method: 1) 提出自动流程，利用现成的注视估计器从野外视频中提取多样化 gaze/head 运动作为训练数据；2) 构建生成式条件变分自编码器（conditional VAE）建模 gaze-head 协调的概率相关性与时序动态；3) 将框架应用于注视控制的面部视频生成，生成与输入注视自然相关的头部运动。

Result: 人类评估和定量比较表明方法有效，统计显著地优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 验证了数据驱动方法建模 gaze-head 协调的可行性，展示了在注视控制视频生成中的有效性，强调了注视与头部运动自然相关性的重要性。

Abstract: We present the first data-driven approach to model temporal gaze-head coordination from large-scale in-the-wild facial videos. To obtain training data for generalizable learning, we propose an automatic pipeline that extracts natural yet diverse gaze and head motions with off-the-shelf appearance-based gaze estimators. To capture the probabilistic correlation and temporal dynamics of gaze-head coordination, we build our model on a generative conditional Variational Autoencoder for plausible yet diverse gaze-conditioned head motion generations. We further apply our framework to gaze-controlled facial video generation, where we enable video generation with natural and realistic head motion correlated to the input gaze - an aspect that has not been emphasized before. Human evaluation and quantitative comparisons demonstrate our method's effectiveness and validate our design choices, with evaluators showing statistically significant preference for our approach over baseline methods.

</details>


### [164] [[CLS] is Not Enough: Multi-Label Recognition via Patch-Level Inference and Adaptive Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25821)
*Akang Wang,Xili Deng,Zhanxuan Hu,Yi Zhao,Yonghang Tai,Huafeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PIAA是一个无需训练的多标签图像识别框架，通过补丁级推理和自适应聚合，在NUS-WIDE基准上相比代表性基线提升了超过6%的mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）在零样本识别中表现优异，但在多标签识别任务中表现不佳，因为单个全局[CLS]标记无法充分编码具有不同尺度、上下文和共现模式的多个目标。

Method: 提出PIAA框架，包含两个关键部分：(i) 从两个互补角度增强补丁级预测——减轻视觉编码器中的语义纠缠以获得更具判别性的补丁表示，以及学习无监督视觉分类器以缩小视觉-语言模态差距；(ii) 引入自适应聚合模块，将补丁级分数整合为最终的多标签预测。整个流程无需训练，不需要梯度更新或参数微调。

Result: 在NUS-WIDE基准上，相比代表性基线实现了超过6%的mAP提升，且仅需极少的额外计算开销。

Conclusion: PIAA通过补丁级推理和自适应聚合有效解决了多标签识别中全局表示不足的问题，验证了无需训练即可显著提升多标签识别性能的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models such as CLIP exhibit strong zero-shot recognition capability by aligning images with textual concepts, yet they often underperform on multi-label recognition where multiple objects co-exist. A key bottleneck is that the [CLS] token, as a single global visual representation, is insufficient to faithfully encode diverse targets with varying scales, contexts, and co-occurrence patterns. To address this limitation, we present a new multi-label image recognition framework, termed PIAA, which formulates prediction as Patch-level Inference followed by Adaptive Aggregation. Specifically, we first enhance patch-wise predictions from two complementary perspectives: (i) mitigating semantic entanglement in the visual encoder to obtain more discriminative patch representations, and (ii) learning an unsupervised visual classifier to narrow the vision-language modality gap. We then introduce an adaptive aggregation module that consolidates patch-level scores into the final multi-label prediction. Notably, the entire pipeline is fully training-free, requiring no gradient updates or parameter fine-tuning. Experiments show that our method achieves strong improvements with minimal extra computation, exceeding a 6% mAP gain on the challenging NUS-WIDE benchmark over representative baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/akang-wang/PIAA.

</details>


### [165] [SAM3-Assisted Training of Lightweight YOLO Models for Precision Pig Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25860)
*Marcos Vinicius Mendes Faria,Thiago Borges Pereira,Isabella C. F. S. Condotta,Thiago Meireles Paixão,Francisco de Assis Boldt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种完全自动化的知识蒸馏流程，利用SAM 3模型为零样本生成伪标签来训练轻量级YOLOv8检测器，消除人工标注瓶颈，在PigLife数据集上达到79.4% mAP且推理延迟降低约200倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度基础模型（如SAM 3）计算量过大无法边缘部署，而轻量模型（如YOLO）需要大量人工标注，二者之间存在关键瓶颈。

Method: 将SAM 3作为离线自动标注器生成零样本伪标签，通过知识蒸馏训练YOLOv8检测器，实现无需人工干预的全自动化流程。

Result: SAM 3训练的YOLOv8m达到79.4% mAP，推理延迟较教师模型降低约200倍；低遮挡场景下AP50超过99%，与人工标注基准相当。

Conclusion: 基础模型可作为有效的零标注成本监督者，为智慧农业提供可扩展的边缘计算解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning-based object detection has revolutionized Precision Livestock Farming (PLF), yet a critical barrier remains: high-performance Foundation Models (such as SAM 3) are too computationally intensive for edge deployment, while lightweight models (like YOLO) require prohibitive manual annotation efforts. This work proposes a fully automated knowledge distillation pipeline that leverages the Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) to generate zero-shot pseudo-labels for training efficient YOLOv8 detectors. By treating SAM 3 as an offline auto-annotator, we eliminate the manual labeling bottleneck, producing models capable of real-time inference on resource-constrained hardware. We systematically evaluate this approach on the PigLife dataset, comparing SAM 3-supervised models against human-annotated baselines. Results demonstrate that a SAM 3-trained YOLOv8m achieves a mean Average Precision (mAP) of 79.4% without human intervention, while reducing inference latency by approximately 200$\times$ compared to the teacher model. Furthermore, stratified analysis reveals that in low-occlusion scenarios, the automated pipeline achieves detection rates comparable to human benchmarks ($AP_{50} > 99\%$). These findings indicate that foundation models can serve as effective, zero-annotation-cost supervisors, enabling scalable edge computing solutions for smart agriculture.

</details>


### [166] [WBench: A Comprehensive Multi-turn Benchmark for Interactive Video World Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25874)
*Kaining Ying,Hengrui Hu,Siyu Ren,Jiamu Li,Fengjiao Chen,Ziwen Wang,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai,Henghui Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WBench是一个用于评估交互式世界模型的综合多轮基准测试，包含289个测试用例和1,058个交互轮次，从视频质量、设定遵循、交互遵循、一致性和物理合规五个维度进行评估，覆盖20个SOTA模型的实验表明没有模型在所有维度上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式世界模型发展迅速，但缺乏统一的系统评估标准，已有基准仅覆盖部分能力，因此需要构建一个全面的多维度评估基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建WBench基准，包含289个测试用例和1,058个交互轮次，每个用例包含世界设定和多轮交互序列；覆盖多种场景、风格、主体、视角及四种交互类型（导航、主体动作、事件编辑、视角切换）；统一文本、6-DoF位姿和离散动作控制；使用22个自动子指标（结合专业视觉模型与大模型）并经过人工验证；在20个SOTA模型上进行评估。

Result: 在20个SOTA模型上的评估显示，没有单一模型在所有五个维度上表现优异；提供了各模型特征性优势和劣势的详细诊断分析。

Conclusion: WBench为交互式世界模型提供了系统化的多维度评估框架，揭示了当前模型在不同能力维度上的差异和挑战，为后续研究提供了诊断性指导。

Abstract: Interactive world models are advancing rapidly, yet existing benchmarks cover only part of the required competencies, leaving no unified standard for systematic evaluation. To fill this gap, we introduce WBench, a comprehensive multi-turn benchmark for interactive world model evaluation along five dimensions, namely video quality, setting adherence, interaction adherence, consistency, and physics compliance. WBench contains 289 test cases and 1,058 interaction turns, where each case specifies a world setting and a multi-turn interaction sequence, covering diverse scenes, styles, subjects, and both first- and third-person perspectives, together with four interaction types, including navigation, subject action, event editing, and perspective switching. For navigation, WBench unifies text, 6-DoF pose, and discrete-action control, enabling evaluation of models with different native input interfaces. Evaluation uses 22 automatic sub-metrics that combine specialist vision models with large multimodal models, and all metrics are validated against human judgments. Across 20 state-of-the-art models, we find that no single model performs strongly across all dimensions. We provide detailed diagnostic insights into the characteristic strengths, weaknesses, and open challenges of each model. Code and data are available at https://github.com/meituan-longcat/WBench.

</details>


### [167] [DyCoRM: Dynamic Criterion-Aware Reward Modeling for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25876)
*Jiaying Qian,Ziheng Jia,Qian Zhang,Zicheng Zhang,Jiayi Guo,Junqi Zhang,Guangtao Zhai,Xiongkuo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DyCoRM，一种动态、准则感知的奖励模型，用于在文本到图像生成中根据用户特定的细粒度准则评估图像，并构建了DyCoDataset-20K数据集和DyCoBench-1K基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着T2I生成质量提升，用户需求转向更精细的特定要求。现有奖励模型依赖静态通用评估维度，无法捕捉用户任务相关的细粒度准则，因此需要动态、准则感知的奖励建模。

Method: 提出DyCoRM（动态准则感知奖励模型），包含基础任务相关准则和准则感知偏好比较；构建DyCoDataset-20K（含动态准则及准则级标注）和DyCoBench-1K基准；引入DyCoPick用于准则感知的T2I图像选择。

Result: 建立了首个面向动态细粒度评估的奖励建模框架，并在T2I生成中进行了实际应用验证。

Conclusion: DyCoRM为动态、细粒度的奖励评估提供了首个系统性框架，填补了现有奖励模型在任务相关准则感知方面的空白，并展示了在实际T2I生成中的有效性。

Abstract: With the continued advancement of text-to-image (T2I) generation, producing high-quality images is becoming increasingly attainable; consequently, user demands are shifting toward images that better satisfy their specific requirements. As reward models play an increasingly important role in assessing whether generated images align with user preference, this trend introduces an important challenge for reward modeling: rather than relying solely on static and general evaluation dimensions, reward models should account for the task-relevant and fine-grained criteria through which users assess whether generated images meet their specific requirements. To address this challenge, we propose DyCoRM, a dynamic, criterion-aware reward model that grounds task-relevant criteria and performs criterion-aware preference comparison. To support this setting, we construct DyCoDataset-20K, which provides dynamic criteria together with criterion-level annotations, and further derive DyCoBench-1K, a benchmark for systematically evaluating reward models under dynamic criteria. We further introduce DyCoPick, which applies criterion-aware reward modeling to selecting T2I images. Our contributions establish the first reward modeling framework for dynamic and fine-grained evaluation and practical application in T2I generation.

</details>


### [168] [AgentGrounder: Zero-Shot 3D Visual Pointcloud Grounding using Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25901)
*Cuong Huynh,Maxim Popov,Denis Gridusov,Sergey Kolyubin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgentGrounder是一个零样本3D视觉定位框架，通过离线构建对象查找表（OLT）和在线工具驱动智能体，结合选择性检索、几何推理和自适应视觉检查，在无需任务特定3D训练的情况下实现了对彩色点云的3D目标定位，在ScanRefer和Nr3D基准上优于SeeGround，分别提升+2.5%和+6.3%的Acc@0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本3DVG方法依赖多视图图像和标准3D分割工具，存在语义和空间细节有限的问题，且固定锚点-目标匹配流程会导致级联匹配错误及上下文窗口效率低下。

Method: 两阶段设计：(1)离线阶段：利用3D模型构建包含实例ID、语义标签和3D边界框的对象查找表（OLT）；(2)在线阶段：智能体分解查询，从OLT中检索相关候选，进行几何评分，并在需要额外视觉证据时按需触发图像渲染。

Result: 在零样本设置下，ScanRefer上Acc@0.5提升+2.5%，Nr3D上提升+6.3%，其中视图无关查询提升+6.3%。

Conclusion: 选择性检索、几何推理与自适应视觉检查的结合为开放词汇3D定位提供了实用且鲁棒的基础。

Abstract: 3D Visual Grounding (3DVG) is an essential capability for embodied AI, requiring agents to localize objects in 3D scenes based on natural language descriptions. Recent zero-shot methods leverage 2D vision-language models (LVLMs). However, they often rely on existing sets of multi-view images and struggle with the limited semantic and spatial details provided by standard 3D segmentation tools. We present $\textbf{AgentGrounder}$, a zero-shot 3D visual grounding framework that operates directly on colored point clouds without task-specific 3D training. Our approach follows a two-stage design: (1) an offline stage that applies 3D model to build an Object Lookup Table (OLT) with instance IDs, semantic labels, 3D bounding boxes; and (2) an online tool-driven agent that decomposes each query, retrieves only relevant candidates from the OLT, performs geometric scoring, and triggers image rendering on demand when additional visual evidence (e.g., color, material, or viewpoint-sensitive cues) is required. Compared with fixed anchor-target matching pipelines, this design reduces cascading matching errors and improves context-window efficiency by avoiding prompts overloaded with irrelevant objects. We evaluate on ScanRefer and Nr3D under a zero-shot setting and observe consistent improvements over SeeGround in our setup, including +2.5% Acc@0.5 on ScanRefer and +6.3% on Nr3D, with a notable +6.3% gain on Nr3D view-independent queries. These results show that combining selective retrieval, geometric reasoning, and adaptive visual inspection yields a practical and robust foundation for open-vocabulary 3D grounding. Our code is available at https://github.com/be2rlab/AgentGrounder.

</details>


### [169] [R5DGS: Semantic-Aware 4D Gaussian Splatting with Rigid Body Constraints for Efficient Dynamic Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25909)
*Denis Gridusov,Maxim Popov,Sergey Kolyubin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: R5DGS提出了一种结合身份编码和物理动力学的4D高斯表示方法，通过CLIP查找表实现开放词汇的物体级查询与渲染，并在外推时通过刚体推理约束达到11 FPS的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息高斯泼溅方法具备未来帧外推能力，但缺乏语义感知且计算开销大。

Method: 在物理驱动的4D高斯表示中引入紧凑的身份编码向量实现高斯到物体的关联；构建离线CLIP物体查找表支持开放词汇文本提示检索；提出刚体推理约束，仅预测和集成物体质心的物理动力学，通过相对变换将运动传播到关联高斯。

Result: 在外推阶段实现11 FPS的速度提升，且不牺牲轨迹的合理性。

Conclusion: R5DGS同时实现了语义感知、高效计算和物理可信的动态场景重建与预测。

Abstract: Reconstructing and predicting dynamic 3D scenes from multi-view videos is a foundational task for robotics, AR/VR, and digital twins. Recent physics-informed Gaussian Splatting methods achieve impressive future frame extrapolation but lack semantic awareness and suffer from large computational overhead. We introduce $\textbf{R5DGS}$, a framework that augments a physics-driven 4D Gaussian representation with compact Identity Encoding vectors, enabling precise Gaussian-to-object association. By constructing an offline CLIP-based object lookup table, we support open-vocabulary text prompting to retrieve and render object-specific Gaussians across arbitrary timestamps and viewpoints. Furthermore, we propose a rigid-body inference constraint that predicts and integrates physical dynamics exclusively for object centroids, propagating motion to associated Gaussians via relative transformations. This optimization yields a 11 FPS speedup during extrapolation without compromising trajectories plausibility.

</details>


### [170] [Closed-Loop Bidirectional Prompting for Adversarial Robustness of Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25922)
*Xiao Liu,Jiaxiang Liu,Boci Peng,Boren Hu,Yusong Wang,Xiwen Chen,Prayag Tiwari,Liming Zhang,Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出闭环双向提示（Closed-Loop Bidirectional Prompting）方法，通过动态反馈循环和语义锚点（Semantic Anchor）实现跨模态语义对齐恢复，在冻结编码器上实现鲁棒自适应防御，在11个数据集上达到SOTA鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性防御方法多为单向或结构性的，未能利用双向跨模态互补性和实例级自适应保护，导致视觉语言模型在面对跨模态语义对齐破坏的对抗扰动时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出闭环双向提示框架，引入语义锚点作为稳定先验约束循环更新，通过锚点引导的bootstrap机制：文本语义去噪视觉表征，精炼后的视觉特征实现实例自适应提示更新，在冻结编码器上形成整流后的鲁棒共识。

Result: 在11个数据集上取得SOTA鲁棒性，具备强基类到新类泛化能力，在计算成本与准确率之间实现良好权衡。

Conclusion: 闭环双向提示通过双向跨模态互补和实例自适应保护，有效克服了单向静态防御的局限，为视觉语言模型对抗鲁棒自适应提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision Language Models adapt well to downstream tasks but are highly vulnerable to adversarial perturbations that disrupt cross-modal semantic alignment. Existing defenses are largely unidirectional or structural, failing to exploit bidirectional cross-modal complementarity and instance-wise adaptive protection. To overcome the limitations of unidirectional and static defenses in adversarial settings, we propose Closed-Loop Bidirectional Prompting, casting robust adaptation as cross-modal agreement recovery via a dynamic feedback loop on frozen encoders. A Semantic Anchor is introduced as a stable prior to constrain cyclic updates and mitigate perturbation-induced feature corruption. Through anchor-based bootstrapping, textual semantics denoise visual representations, while the refined visuals enable instance-adaptive prompt updating, yielding a rectified and robust consensus. Extensive evaluations across 11 datasets validate state-of-the-art robustness and strong base-to-new generalization, while maintaining a favorable trade-off between computational cost and accuracy.

</details>


### [171] [Where Concept Erasure Should Occur: Concept-Layer Alignment in Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25941)
*Yiwei Xie,Ping Liu,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现文本到视频扩散Transformer在不同深度层对语义信息编码不均，提出概念-层拓扑对齐瓶颈。基于此，引入CLEAR框架，通过分离性驱动的优化实现概念擦除，在保持生成质量的同时更精确地抑制目标概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除方法在文本到视频扩散模型中效果受限，原因是模型不同深度层对语义信息的编码不均匀，导致概念与非目标信号在大多数深度层中高度纠缠，限制了逐层擦除的有效性。

Method: 提出CLEAR框架，将概念擦除重构为识别概念-非目标自然分离的表示深度的问题。通过将层选择建模为基于概念-非目标可分离性的优化问题，引入分离性感知目标函数，选择概念-非目标分离更强的层进行擦除。

Result: 在大规模文本到视频扩散模型上的实验表明，强制概念-层对齐能够实现更精确的概念抑制，同时保持整体生成质量。

Conclusion: 概念擦除的效果依赖于选择合适的表示深度层，通过分离性驱动的层选择优化可以显著提升擦除精度，为深度生成模型中的概念编辑提供了新的结构性视角。

Abstract: Text-to-video diffusion transformers encode semantic information unevenly across model depth, which constrains effective concept erasure. We identify a representational bottleneck, termed concept-layer topological alignment, under which target concepts exhibit higher separability at certain representational depths. Outside these depths, concept and non-target signals remain strongly entangled, limiting the effectiveness of depth-specific erasure. This observation reframes concept erasure as the problem of identifying representational depths where concept-non-target separation naturally emerges. Motivated by this structural constraint, we introduce CLEAR, a separability-driven optimization framework for concept erasure that explicitly enforces concept-layer alignment. CLEAR operationalizes this principle by formulating layer selection as an optimization problem over concept-non-target separability, rather than relying on layer-agnostic or heuristic choices. To enable this, we introduce a separability-aware objective that favors layers exhibiting stronger concept-non-target separation. Experiments on large-scale text-to-video diffusion models demonstrate that enforcing concept--layer alignment leads to more precise concept suppression while preserving overall generative quality.

</details>


### [172] [LRDDv3: High-Resolution Long-Range Drone Detection Dataset with Range Information and Thermal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25942)
*Knut Peterson,Zaid Mayers,Azmain Yousuf,Priontu Chowdhury,Asher Zaczepinski,Solmaz Arezoomandan,Reihaneh Maarefdoust,David Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个高分辨率、长距离的无人机检测数据集，包含102,532张RGB图像和29,630张配套的IR图像，支持在共享空域中实现长距离无人机检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人机在各种空域中的普及，有人和无人飞行器都需要能够在远距离检测无人机以确保安全运行。现有无人机检测数据集在高分辨率长距离数据方面仍然不足。

Method: 收集了128个不同视频片段中的102,532张长距离RGB图像（5 FPS采样），以及29,630张IR图像，所有图像均为4K分辨率，搭配640x512 IR图像。数据在17天内采集，跨越8个月，涵盖多样光照、地点和背景。

Result: 构建了一个包含完整距离信息的、高分辨率（4K RGB + 640x512 IR）的长距离无人机检测数据集。

Conclusion: 该数据集是首批利用4K分辨率和配套IR图像进行长距离无人机检测的数据集之一，为提升共享空域中无人机检测能力提供了重要支持。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) have quickly become common in various airspaces, representing a wide range of applications from recreation flying to commercial photography and package delivery. With the increasing prevalence of UAVs, it becomes critical that both manned and unmanned aircraft can detect UAVs and other flying objects from long range to effectively track movement and ensure safe operation in shared spaces. While several datasets have been introduced for drone detection, the need for expanded high-quality data persists, especially in the area of high-resolution long-range drone data. To address this, we introduce a high-resolution dataset of 102,532 long-range RGB images of drones, sampled at 5 FPS from 128 distinct video clips taken mid flight during 17 different data collection days spread over 8 months to ensure a wide variety of lighting scenarios, flight locations, and background elements. The dataset boasts comprehensive drone range information across the dataset, as well as 29,630 IR images, all paired with RGB counterparts from the base dataset. As one of the first drone detection datasets to leverage 4K image resolution and paired 640x512 IR images, our work represents a significant advancement to enable the detection of drones at long range. For access to the complete dataset, please visit https://research.coe.drexel.edu/ece/imaple/lrddv3/

</details>


### [173] [EchoPilot: Training-Free Ultrasound Video Segmentation via Scale-Space Semantic Prompting and Reliability-Gated Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25944)
*Ruiqiang Xiao,Zhaohu Xing,Yijun Yang,Zhenyan Han,Weiming Wang,Kaishun Wu,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoPilot是一个无需训练的超声视频分割框架，仅需单点点击和解剖类别名称即可实现高质量分割。它通过尺度空间语义提示解决初始化模糊问题，通过可靠性门控记忆更新减少传播漂移，在三个超声视频数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声视频分割因斑点噪声、弱边界和快速解剖变形而困难。现有可提示基础模型在超声上不可靠：单点提供空间上下文不足导致尺度模糊，贪婪记忆更新将早期错误放大为严重时间漂移。

Method: EchoPilot编排一个冻结的医学视觉-语言模型（VLM）进行语义定位、一个视觉基础模型（VFM）提取密集几何特征、以及一个可提示视频分割器进行掩码预测和传播。提出尺度空间语义提示（通过S.E.E.D.准则选择最优上下文视图并合成辅助点提示）和可靠性门控记忆更新（在不确定预测下选择性冻结记忆库以防止误差累积）。

Result: 在三个超声视频数据集上，EchoPilot在稀疏交互设置下达到最先进性能，持续优于无需训练的基线和微调专家模型。同时贡献了首个动态胎儿胎盘超声视频分割数据集，包含671个标注帧。

Conclusion: EchoPilot通过无需训练的框架有效解决了超声视频分割中初始化模糊和传播漂移两大核心难题，验证了多基础模型协同工作的潜力。

Abstract: Ultrasound video segmentation is clinically valuable yet difficult due to speckle noise, weak boundaries, and rapid anatomical deformation. Recent promptable foundation models enable point-guided segmentation, but their direct deployment in ultrasound remains unreliable: a single point provides insufficient spatial context to resolve scale ambiguity, and greedy memory updates amplify early errors into severe temporal drift. We present EchoPilot, a training-free framework for ultrasound video segmentation under sparse first-frame interaction, requiring only a single point click and an anatomical category name. EchoPilot orchestrates a frozen medical vision-language model (VLM) for semantic localization, a vision foundation model (VFM) for dense geometric feature extraction, and a promptable video segmentor for mask prediction and propagation. To resolve initialization ambiguity, we propose Scale-Space Semantic Prompting, which first selects an optimal contextual view via a parameter-free S.E.E.D. (Semantic Energy-Entropy Density) criterion, and then synthesizes geometrically precise auxiliary point prompts from dense foundation features without additional user interaction. To reduce propagation drift, a Reliability-Gated Memory update is further introduced to selectively freeze the segmentor's memory bank under uncertain predictions, preventing error accumulation. We also contribute the first dynamic fetal placenta ultrasound video segmentation dataset with 671 annotated frames. Across three ultrasound video datasets, EchoPilot achieves state-of-the-art performance under the sparse-interactive setting, consistently outperforming training-free baselines and finetuned specialists.

</details>


### [174] [A Pedestrian-Vehicle Interaction Benchmark and Annotation Framework for Unstructured Scenes via Uncalibrated Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25947)
*Haoyang Peng,Qian Hu,Songan Zhang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于未标定监控摄像头的行人-车辆交互数据集标注框架，构建了PINNS数据集，覆盖多国多场景的密集行人-车辆交互轨迹数据，旨在促进非结构化场景下的轨迹预测和自动驾驶研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开数据集缺乏密集行人-车辆交互场景，且大多基于结构化道路数据，非结构化环境中的复杂异构交互研究不足，限制了自动驾驶在非结构化场景中的安全性。

Method: 提出基于未标定监控摄像头视频的数据集标注框架，构建PINNS数据集，涵盖多国多地区、多季节、多光照和多天气条件的典型交通场景，按照中国自动化学会标准进行构建和标注，提供轨迹数据和场景级信息。

Result: 构建了PINNS数据集，覆盖密集行人-车辆交互的复杂场景，具备易扩展性，提供标准化的轨迹和场景级标注数据。

Conclusion: PINNS数据集能有效支持异构智能体轨迹预测和非结构化混合交通场景下的自动驾驶研究，填补了该领域的数据空白。

Abstract: Predicting the interaction between pedestrian and vehicle is essential for autonomous driving safety in unstructured and semi-structured scenarios; however, this task is severely hindered by the scarcity of public datasets that feature dense pedestrian-vehicle interactions. Most current studies rely on structured road data, leaving the complex, heterogeneous interactions found in unstructured environments insufficiently represented and researched. In this paper, we propose a dataset annotation framework based on video data from uncalibrated surveillance cameras and present PINNS (Pedestrian-vehicle Interaction dataset from uNcalibrated cameras in uNstructured Scenes). The dataset covers multiple countries and regions, includes diverse typical traffic scenarios, and considers variations in seasons, lighting conditions, and weather. It focuses on complex scenes with dense pedestrian-vehicle interactions and is designed to be easily extensible. The dataset is constructed and annotated according to the standard issued by the Chinese Association of Automation, providing both trajectory data and corresponding scene-level information. Furthermore, this paper analyzes current challenges and research directions in heterogeneous agent trajectory prediction, shows the necessity and usefulness of the proposed dataset. We hope our framework and dataset will facilitate research on trajectory prediction and autonomous driving in complex mixed traffic scenarios. PINNS is publicly available at https://github.com/Songan-Lab.

</details>


### [175] [VEN-VL: A Visual Ensemble MoE Framework for Effective and Efficient Multi-Modal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25952)
*Yinghao Wu,Zhuoyan Luo,Yiyao Yu,Zhaojian Yu,Yujiu Yang,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有高效多模态理解方法因过度压缩单一视觉线索和依赖启发式剪枝策略导致性能下降的问题，提出VEN-VL视觉集成MoE框架，通过"先丰富后压缩"原则统一多视角视觉表示、自适应路由压缩信息，并引入显式视觉监督保留关键信息，在少量压缩token下显著提升复杂视觉任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效多模态方法在加速理解时存在明显的性能下降，根本原因在于过度追求单一视觉线索的高压缩比，以及依赖基于粗略注意力对齐的启发式剪枝策略，导致视觉token的信息容量和密度成为瓶颈。

Method: 1) 信息容量丰富：统一不同视角的视觉表示；2) 信息密度压缩：通过自适应路由器在专业视觉专家中逐步压缩；3) 显式视觉监督：引入原始结构的重建能力促进关键信息保留。

Result: 在复杂视觉任务中，使用少量信息压缩的token即取得优越性能，有效弥合了性能与效率之间的差距。

Conclusion: VEN-VL通过"先丰富后压缩"的视觉集成MoE框架，结合显式视觉监督，成功在高效推理和强性能之间取得平衡，验证了提升视觉token信息容量与密度是突破当前高效多模态方法瓶颈的有效途径。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable progress achieved by recent efficient methods in accelerating multimodal understanding, they still suffer from noticeable performance degradation. Their emphasis on the high compression ratio of a single visual clue and reliance on the heuristic pruning strategy with coarse attention alignment incurs a bottleneck on the information capacity and density of visual tokens. Addressing this limitation, we propose VEN-VL, a visual ensemble MoE framework for effective and efficient perception following the enrich then compact principle. Specifically, we first enrich the information capacity by unifying the visual representations of different perspectives, and then progressively compact it with adaptive routers in specialized visual experts to enhance the information density. Furthermore, we incorporate the reconstruction ability of vanilla structure via explicit visual supervision, facilitating crucial information preservation. Experimental results demonstrate our superiority in complex visual tasks with few information-condensed tokens, which effectively bridges the gap between performance and efficiency.

</details>


### [176] [RAPTOR+: A Visually Grounded Vision-Language Framework to Improve Clinical Trust and Auditability in Automated Cancer Referral Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25956)
*Sofiat Abioye,Ufaq Khan,Shazad Ashraf,Anusha Jose,Benjamin Wallace,William Poulett,Adam Byfield,Lukman Akanbi,Muhammad Bilal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RAPTOR+ 是一种多模态扩展系统，使用视觉语言模型（VLM）对疑似结直肠癌紧急转诊文书进行端到端理解，通过任务特定微调显著提升了提取准确率和可验证的证据定位能力，其中微调后的 Qwen3-VL-8B 在阅读准确率（96.1%）和严格安全性（60.6%）上均大幅优于零样本模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧急疑似结直肠癌（CRC）转诊文书多为半结构化临床文档，传统依赖手动审阅和转录，造成操作瓶颈；原有的 RAPTOR 系统虽使用大语言模型进行结构化提取，但依赖独立的 OCR 阶段，易受手写、布局变化影响，且丢失视觉证据关联。

Method: 提出 RAPTOR+ 多模态扩展，使用视觉语言模型（VLM）实现端到端转诊理解；在223份临床整理的CRC紧急转诊表格上评估了微调VLM、商业和开源零样本VLM以及原始OCR流水线；并引入了一个基于定位（grounding-aware）的评估框架，同时衡量提取准确性和证据定位能力。

Result: 零样本模型存在明显的定位差距：Gemini 2.5 Flash 阅读准确率92.6%，但严格安全性仅1.2%；微调的 Qwen3-VL-8B 阅读准确率达96.1%，严格安全性达60.6%，显著提升了可验证的证据定位能力。

Conclusion: 任务特定微调对于实现可靠、可审计的临床文档理解至关重要；RAPTOR+ 能够将提取的转诊决策与视觉证据链接，支持更安全、更高效的癌症转诊分诊。

Abstract: Urgent suspected colorectal cancer (CRC) referrals create operational bottlenecks because semi-structured clinical documents often require manual review and transcription. The original RAPTOR system used Large Language Models for structured extraction but relied on a separate OCR stage, making it vulnerable to handwriting, layout variation, and loss of visual evidence linkage. We present RAPTOR+, a multimodal extension that uses Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for end-to-end referral understanding. We evaluate fine-tuned VLMs, commercial and open-source zero-shot VLMs, and the original OCR-based pipeline on 223 clinically curated CRC urgent referral forms. We also introduce a grounding-aware evaluation framework that measures both extraction accuracy and evidence localisation. Results show a clear grounding gap in zero-shot models. Gemini 2.5 Flash achieved 92.6% Reading Accuracy but only 1.2% Strict Safety. In contrast, fine-tuned Qwen3-VL-8B achieved 96.1% Reading Accuracy and 60.6% Strict Safety, substantially improving verifiable evidence grounding. These findings show that task-specific fine-tuning is essential for reliable, auditable clinical document understanding. RAPTOR+ enables extracted referral decisions to be linked to visual evidence, supporting safer and more efficient cancer referral triage.

</details>


### [177] [Context-driven Missing-Modality Learning for Robust Medical Diagnosis with Image-Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25968)
*Tianling Liu,Lequan Yu,Tong Han,Liang Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对临床多模态数据任意缺失导致模型性能退化的问题，提出CMML框架（上下文驱动的缺失模态学习），通过级联残差Transformer自编码器（CRTA）合成缺失模态，再经语义对齐解决异质性差异，最终在皮肤、眼病、脑膜瘤三个数据集上超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践中多模态数据（影像+表格）常出现任意模态缺失，现有方法要么直接丢弃缺失模态造成信息损失，要么难以在捕捉复杂跨模态依赖的同时合成缺失模态，亟需一种能鲁棒处理任意缺失条件的多模态诊断框架。

Method: 提出CMML框架，包含两个核心阶段：（1）模态合成阶段——设计级联残差Transformer自编码器（CRTA），利用可学习上下文令牌作为数据集级语义先验捕获跨模态依赖，通过模态特定记忆库丰富合成表示；（2）语义对齐阶段——将上下文令牌转化为实例自适应语义参考，引导异构模态表示对齐到统一空间，并应用类感知对比精炼挖掘判别性诊断线索。

Result: 在皮肤病变（Derm7pt）、眼病（ODIR）、脑膜瘤（MEN）三个数据集上，AVG AUC分别提升1.26%、0.97%、1.32%，显著优于当前SOTA方法。

Conclusion: CMML通过上下文驱动的缺失模态合成与语义对齐，有效解决了任意模态缺失下的鲁棒诊断问题，验证了利用数据集级语义先验和实例自适应对齐策略的有效性。

Abstract: While multimodal data integrating diverse imaging and clinical tabular records is crucial for accurate medical diagnosis, the arbitrary absence of specific modalities is prevalent in clinical practice, severely degrading the performance of multimodal models. Existing methods either discard missing modalities, leading to information loss, or struggle to synthesize them without capturing complex inter-modal dependencies. To address these limitations, we propose a novel Context-driven Missing-Modality Learning (CMML) framework, which sequentially performs modality synthesis and semantic alignment to achieve robust diagnosis under arbitrary missing conditions. Specifically, we design a Cascade Residual Transformer-based Autoencoder (CRTA) that leverages learnable context tokens acting as dataset-level semantic prior to capture inter-modal dependencies and synthesize key missing representations. These representations are further enriched by modality-specific memory banks. To resolve the discrepancy between original available and synthesized representations, we transform the learned context tokens into instance-adaptive semantic references by infusing multimodal representations from the CRTA's outputs. This reference guides the alignment of heterogeneous modality representations into a unified space, where class-aware contrastive refinement is finally applied to explore discriminative diagnostic cues. Extensive evaluations on skin lesion (Derm7pt), ocular disease (ODIR), and meningioma (MEN) datasets demonstrate that CMML significantly outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, yielding AVG AUC improvements of 1.26%, 0.97%, and 1.32%, respectively.

</details>


### [178] [LLaVA-OneVision-2: Towards Next-Generation Perceptual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25979)
*Xiang An,Yin Xie,Feilong Tang,Yunyao Yan,Huajie Tan,Didi Zhu,Changrui Chen,Xiuwei Zhao,Bin Qin,Kaicheng Yang,Yifei Shen,Yuanhan Zhang,Kaichen Zhang,Wenkang Zhang,Zheng Cheng,Nansen Zhang,Chunsheng Wu,Chunjiang Ge,Zimin Ran,Dehua Song,Chunyuan Li,Shikun Feng,Ming Hu,Zhangquan Chen,Junbo Niu,Bo Li,Ziyong Feng,Ziwei Liu,Zongyuan Ge,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LLaVA-OV-2 是 LLaVA-OneVision 系列的最新视觉语言模型，通过编码器-流分词器将压缩视频视为连续位成本流，利用位成本动态和运动残差进行自适应时空分组与压缩，实现长视频高效令牌分配，在多项视频、空间和跟踪基准上显著超越 Qwen3-VL-8B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在处理长视频时面临令牌预算有限、不同视频内容的信息密度不均等问题，固定分组方法（GOP）无法根据内容动态调整令牌分配，导致事件承载信息被稀释。此外，现有视频评估缺乏针对高频密集重复动作的细粒度时序定位基准。

Method: 提出编码器-流分词器（codec-stream tokenization），将压缩视频视为连续位成本流；利用位成本动态确定自适应时序分组，运动残差线索选择显著空间证据构建紧凑视觉画布；采用共享 3D RoPE 将编码器画布、采样帧和图像统一到同一时空坐标系；引入 Windowed Attention 保持原生分辨率下的高效局部计算；构建约 8M 重标注视频样本和 4M 空间样本的训练栈。

Result: 在 JumpScore 基准上，LLaVA-OV-2-8B 达到 74.9 mAP，远超 Qwen3-VL-8B 的 30.1（+44.8）；相同令牌预算下编码器-流相比帧采样提升时序定位 +9.7 点；标准基准上在视频任务平均超越 +4.3 点，空间任务 +5.3 点，跟踪任务 J&F 平均 +15.6。

Conclusion: 编码器-流分词器通过位成本动态和运动残差实现了比固定分组更稳定的长视频令牌压缩，使模型能够统一处理视频理解、时序/空间定位、操作轨迹推理等多项任务，并大幅刷新现有多模态基准。

Abstract: We introduce LLaVA-OneVision-2 (LLaVA-OV-2), the most capable vision-language model in the LLaVA-OneVision series to date, achieving superior performance across a broad range of multimodal benchmarks. The model builds on a native OneVision-Encoder and incorporates Windowed Attention for efficient local computation while maintaining native resolution. Its key advance is codec-stream tokenization: it treats compressed video as a continuous bit-cost stream, where bit-cost dynamics determine adaptive temporal groups, and motion-residual cues select salient spatial evidence into compact visual canvases. This allocation concentrates a limited token budget on event-bearing content, enabling more stable long-video token compression than fixed groups of pictures. A shared 3D RoPE further places codec canvases, sampled frames, and images in a unified spatiotemporal coordinate system. Furthermore, we build the LLaVA-OV-2 data and training stack around large-scale open supervision: approximately 8M re-captioned video samples for pretraining, a 4M-sample spatial corpus for fine-tuning. We also introduce JumpScore, a temporal-localization benchmark targeting fine-grained grounding in high-frequency, densely repeated motion, a regime underrepresented by existing video evaluations. A standout capability of LLaVA-OV-2 is its unified perception across video understanding, temporal grounding, spatial grounding, and manipulation-trace reasoning. On JumpScore, LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B reaches 74.9 JumpScore mAP, surpassing Qwen3-VL-8B (30.1) by +44.8 points; under matched visual-token budgets on the same benchmark, codec-stream inputs improve temporal grounding over frame sampling by +9.7 points. Across standard benchmarks, LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B further outperforms Qwen3-VL-8B by +4.3 average points on video tasks, +5.3 on spatial tasks, and +15.6 average J&F on tracking tasks.

</details>


### [179] [MAGIC: Multimodal Alignment & Grounding-aware Instruction Coreset for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26004)
*Shristi Das Biswas,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAGIC是一种无需训练的前向核心集选择方法，通过多模态增益、桥接相关性和技能神经元签名三种内在信号，为大规模视觉语言模型构建紧凑且行为保真的指令微调子集，在20%数据量下达到甚至超越全量微调性能，同时降低73.7%的运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模多模态指令微调数据集中存在大量冗余、低视觉依赖以及多模态推理行为覆盖高度不平衡的样本，导致均匀子采样或基于分数的朴素选择方法得到的训练子集效果次优。

Method: MAGIC方法包含三个阶段：1）过滤低多模态增益样本；2）通过归一化质量目标对候选样本排序；3）基于离散神经元签名进行桶式预算分配以保持潜在多模态技能覆盖。采用多模态增益、桥接相关性和技能神经元签名三种自监督信号，无需反向传播、辅助选择器训练或连续激活空间中的聚类。

Result: 在LLaVA-665K和Vision-Flan数据集上，20%预算下MAGIC在LLaVA-1.5-7B和-13B模型上达到全量微调的100.3%和101.6%相对性能，同时减少73.7%的端到端运行时间。

Conclusion: MAGIC是一种高效、无需额外训练的核心集选择方法，能够在显著降低数据量和计算开销的同时，保持甚至提升多模态指令微调的性能表现。

Abstract: Instruction tuning of large vision-language models (LVLMs) increasingly depends on massive multimodal corpora, yet these datasets contain samples with substantial redundancy, low visual dependency, and highly imbalanced coverage of multimodal reasoning behaviors. As a result, uniform subsampling or naive score-based selection often yields suboptimal training subsets. We introduce MAGIC, a training-free, forward-only coreset selection method designed to construct compact yet behaviorally faithful subsets for multimodal instruction tuning. MAGIC is built on three intrinsic signals extracted from a pretrained VLM: Multimodal Gain, which measures the likelihood improvement obtained from visual input; Bridging Relevance, which captures the sharpness of answer-token grounding over visual tokens; and Skill-Neuron Signatures, which characterize the functional computation elicited by each sample via top-activated feed-forward neurons. MAGIC combines these signals in a three-stage pipeline: filtering low-gain examples, ranking candidates by a normalized quality objective, and performing bucket-wise budget allocation over discrete neuron signatures to preserve latent multimodal skill coverage. This formulation avoids backpropagation, auxiliary selector training, and expensive clustering in continuous activation spaces, while remaining efficient and easily deployable in existing VLMs. Across LLaVA-665K and Vision-Flan datasets, and transfer settings to large target models, LLaVA-1.5-7B and -13B, MAGIC consistently improves over strong baselines under matched 20% budgets: it achieves 100.3% relative performance to full finetuning on LLaVA-665K and 101.6% relative performance on Vision-Flan-186K, while yielding a 73.7% reduction in wall-clock run time.

</details>


### [180] [MIND: Multi-Scale Intent Diffusion for Text-Driven Physics-Based Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26006)
*Bin Li,Ruichi Zhang,Han Liang,Jingyan Zhang,Juze Zhang,Xin Chen,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV
